基于世界模型的生成与规划World Model-Based Generation and Planning

基于世界模型的导航规划正从单纯的视觉预测转向具备物理常识与长程推理能力的生成式规划。早期工作如Think2Drive和DriveDreamer-2利用隐变量或LLM增强视频生成，解决了长尾场景数据匮乏问题，但多依赖上帝视角或合成数据。演进中，Vista、NWM和AETHER通过高保真扩散架构与几何感知，提升了模型在真实物理约束下的泛化性与零样本迁移能力。近期研究如WoTE、Alpamayo-R1及UNIVERSE进一步将世界模型从“预测工具”转化为“规划核心”，通过BEV空间压缩、因果推理链及统一视频动作模型，实现了实时轨迹评估与端到端决策的闭环，显著降低了仿真与实车部署的鸿沟。World model-based navigation planning is shifting from mere visual prediction to generative planning endowed with physical common sense and long-horizon reasoning capabilities. Early works such as Think2Drive and DriveDreamer-2 utilized latent variables or LLMs to enhance video generation, addressing the scarcity of long-tail scenario data, yet they often relied on god-view perspectives or synthetic data. In the evolution, Vista, NWM, and AETHER improved model generalization under real physical constraints and zero-shot transfer capabilities through high-fidelity diffusion architectures and geometric perception. Recent studies like WoTE, Alpamayo-R1, and UNIVERSE further transformed world models from "prediction tools" into "planning cores," achieving closed-loop real-time trajectory evaluation and end-to-end decision-making through BEV space compression, causal reasoning chains, and unified video-action models, significantly narrowing the gap between simulation and real-vehicle deployment.

2024-03
DriveDreamer-2: LLM-Enhanced World Models for Diverse Driving Video Generation
动机: Motivation: 应对长尾场景（如鬼探头）数据稀缺问题，利用LLM增强世界模型以生成多样化的驾驶视频数据。Addressing the data scarcity problem in long-tail scenarios (e.g., jaywalking pedestrians), it utilizes LLMs to enhance world models for generating diverse driving video data.
方法: Method: 结合LLM与扩散模型，将文本指令转化为多视角视频，并引入HDMap生成以增强场景逻辑性。Combining LLMs with diffusion models to convert text instructions into multi-view videos, introducing HDMap generation to enhance scene logic.
推进: Advance: 实现了高保真、多样化的长尾场景视频生成，显著提升了下游感知模型在极端场景下的训练效果。Achieved high-fidelity, diverse long-tail scenario video generation, significantly improving the training performance of downstream perception models in extreme scenarios.
2024-05
Vista: A Generalizable Driving World Model with High Fidelity and Versatile Controllability
动机: Motivation: 提升世界模型的高保真度与可控性，使其能直接作为奖励函数辅助自动驾驶规划。Enhancing the high-fidelity and controllability of world models to serve directly as reward functions assisting autonomous driving planning.
方法: Method: 基于SVD魔改构建高保真世界模型，支持10Hz帧率与多模态控制，将模型预测直接用于奖励计算。Constructed a high-fidelity world model based on modified SVD, supporting 10Hz frame rates and multi-modal control, using model predictions directly for reward calculation.
推进: Advance: 实现了高分辨率、高帧率的视频预测与多模态控制，验证了世界模型作为可微分奖励函数的可行性。Achieved high-resolution, high-frame-rate video prediction and multi-modal control, validating the feasibility of world models as differentiable reward functions.
2024-06
Enhancing End-to-End Autonomous Driving with Latent World Model
动机: Motivation: 为端到端自动驾驶提供自监督信号，通过预测未来视觉隐变量来增强特征表示与时空理解。Providing self-supervised signals for end-to-end autonomous driving by predicting future visual latent variables to enhance feature representation and spatio-temporal understanding.
方法: Method: 构建隐变量世界模型预测未来帧特征，将其作为自监督信号蒸馏到感知与规划网络中。Constructing a latent variable world model to predict future frame features, distilling them as self-supervised signals into perception and planning networks.
推进: Advance: 在感知无关与相关框架下均提升了模型性能，证明了时空蒸馏在端到端架构中的通用性与有效性。Improved model performance under both perception-independent and perception-dependent frameworks, proving the universality and effectiveness of spatio-temporal distillation in end-to-end architectures.
2024-12
Navigation World Models
动机: Motivation: 构建具备“做梦”能力的通用具身导航世界模型，以支持未知环境下的长程规划与想象。Constructing a general embodied navigation world model with "dreaming" capabilities to support long-horizon planning and imagination in unknown environments.
方法: Method: 提出CDiT架构的扩散Transformer，线性降低计算复杂度，结合MPC采样进行约束规划。Proposed a diffusion Transformer with CDiT architecture, linearly reducing computational complexity, combined with MPC sampling for constrained planning.
推进: Advance: 实现了高效、高保真的视频预测与规划，证明了扩散模型在具身导航中处理未知环境的潜力。Achieved efficient, high-fidelity video prediction and planning, demonstrating the potential of diffusion models in embodied navigation for handling unknown environments.
2025-04
End-to-End Driving with Online Trajectory Evaluation via BEV World Model
动机: Motivation: 解决端到端驾驶缺乏未来轨迹评估机制的问题，防止模型因短视规划导致事故。Addressing the lack of future trajectory evaluation mechanisms in end-to-end driving to prevent accidents caused by short-sighted planning.
方法: Method: 构建BEV空间世界模型预演未来轨迹，通过评分机制对候选轨迹进行“验毒”与筛选。Constructing a BEV space world model to simulate future trajectories, using a scoring mechanism to "detoxify" and filter candidate trajectories.
推进: Advance: 在保持低延迟的同时实现了实时轨迹评估，显著提升了端到端驾驶的安全性与规划质量。Achieved real-time trajectory evaluation while maintaining low latency, significantly improving the safety and planning quality of end-to-end driving.
2025-11
Alpamayo-R1: Bridging Reasoning and Action Prediction for Generalizable Autonomous Driving in the Long Tail
动机: Motivation: 为自动驾驶注入因果推理能力，解决纯数据驱动模型在长尾场景下“一本正经胡说八道”的问题。Injecting causal reasoning capabilities into autonomous driving to solve the problem of purely data-driven models "talking nonsense seriously" in long-tail scenarios.
方法: Method: 构建因果链（CoC）数据集，利用Cosmos-Reason底座结合RL微调，强制模型学习因果关联。Constructing a Causal Chain (CoC) dataset, utilizing the Cosmos-Reason foundation combined with RL fine-tuning to force the model to learn causal associations.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了模型在长尾场景下的可解释性与泛化能力，验证了因果推理在自动驾驶规划中的关键作用。Significantly improved model interpretability and generalization in long-tail scenarios, validating the critical role of causal reasoning in autonomous driving planning.
2025-12
WorldPlay: Towards Long-Term Geometric Consistency for Real-Time Interactive World Modeling
动机: Motivation: 解决现有世界模型长程记忆缺失与几何不一致问题，实现实时交互式长序列生成。Addressing the lack of long-horizon memory and geometric inconsistency in existing world models, achieving real-time interactive long-sequence generation.
方法: Method: 提出WorldPlay模型，结合FOV检索记忆机制与RoPE重写，实现24FPS下的长序列视频生成。Proposed the WorldPlay model, combining FOV retrieval memory mechanisms and RoPE rewriting to achieve long-sequence video generation at 24FPS.
推进: Advance: 实现了长程几何一致性与实时交互，证明了记忆增强机制在解决Transformer长程衰减问题上的有效性。Achieved long-horizon geometric consistency and real-time interaction, proving the effectiveness of memory-enhanced mechanisms in solving Transformer long-horizon decay problems.
2026-06
AdaJEPA: An Adaptive Latent World Model
动机: Motivation: 针对冻结世界模型在分布偏移下泛化能力不足的问题，旨在探索测试时适应（TTA）在具身规划中的实际效能。Aiming to address the insufficient generalization capability of frozen World Models under distribution shift, this work explores the practical efficacy of Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) in embodied planning.
方法: Method: 提出AdaJEPA，在模型预测控制（MPC）循环中引入单次梯度更新机制，实现模型参数的动态微调。Proposing AdaJEPA, which introduces a single-step gradient update mechanism within the Model Predictive Control (MPC) loop to achieve dynamic fine-tuning of model parameters.
推进: Advance: 证明了无需重新训练即可在有限数据下通过在线适应应对分布偏移，为具身规划提供了轻量级的鲁棒性补丁。Demonstrating that distribution shift can be addressed via online adaptation with limited data without retraining, providing a lightweight robustness patch for embodied planning.
2026-06
IDEA: Insensitive to Dynamics Mismatch via Effect Alignment for Sim-to-Real Transfer in Multi-Agent Control
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决多智能体强化学习中仿真到现实（Sim-to-Real）的“现实差距”问题，特别是动力学参数不匹配带来的挑战。Aiming to solve the Sim-to-Real 'reality gap' problem in multi-agent reinforcement learning, particularly the challenges posed by dynamics parameter mismatch.
方法: Method: 提出IDEA方法，通过效果对齐而非参数对齐，利用高层语义动作在底层闭环控制下强制仿真与现实行为一致。Proposing the IDEA method, which enforces consistency between simulation and real-world behaviors under low-level closed-loop control by aligning effects rather than parameters, utilizing high-level semantic actions.
推进: Advance: 以工程化的同步机制掩盖了底层物理模拟的不足，显著提升了多智能体系统在真实环境中的迁移成功率。Masking deficiencies in underlying physical simulation through an engineered synchronization mechanism, significantly improving the transfer success rate of multi-agent systems in real-world environments.
2026-07
Path Planning in Physically Viable World Models
动机: Motivation: 解决户外机器人在未知地形中缺乏物理可行性预判的问题，实现事前环境模拟。Addressing the lack of physical feasibility prediction for outdoor robots in unknown terrains, achieving pre-event environmental simulation.
方法: Method: 结合高斯泼溅构建静态骨架，引入物理引擎（MPM）模拟洪水滑坡等动态物理过程。Combining Gaussian Splatting to construct static skeletons, introducing physical engines (MPM) to simulate dynamic physical processes such as floods and landslides.
推进: Advance: 实现了基于物理引擎的环境预演，为户外导航提供了“事前诸葛亮”式的可行性评估框架。Achieved environment pre-simulation based on physical engines, providing a "pre-emptive" feasibility assessment framework for outdoor navigation.
2026-07
Traj-VLN: Learning Pixel-Space Interaction via Autoregressive Trajectory Generation
动机: Motivation: 针对视觉语言模型（VLM）在理解3D空间几何时的固有短板，旨在避免复杂的3D显式计算，转而利用2D像素空间进行更直观的交互建模。Aiming to address the inherent limitations of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in understanding 3D spatial geometry, this approach seeks to avoid complex explicit 3D computations and instead utilize 2D pixel space for more intuitive interaction modeling.
方法: Method: 提出Traj-VLN，将导航任务重构为自回归的轨迹生成过程，使模型像绘画一样在像素空间中从近到远逐点描绘路径。Traj-VLN is proposed, reformulating the navigation task as an autoregressive trajectory generation process, enabling the model to draw paths point-by-point from near to far in pixel space, akin to painting.
推进: Advance: 通过强制将3D运动投影至2D平面，显著降低了模型学习空间交互的难度，实现了在特定视角下的SOTA性能，但牺牲了对深度信息的直接感知能力。By enforcing the projection of 3D motion onto a 2D plane, the difficulty of learning spatial interactions is significantly reduced, achieving SOTA performance under specific viewpoints, albeit at the cost of direct depth perception capabilities.
2026-07
NaviAIS: A Scenario-Level Vessel Trajectory Prediction Dataset withVectorized Lane Priors and the NaviLane Forecasting Framework
动机: Motivation: 针对海事导航中AIS数据噪声大且缺乏地图先验的痛点，旨在利用世界模型解决开放海域长视界轨迹预测难题。Aiming to address the pain points of high noise in AIS data and lack of map priors in maritime navigation, this work seeks to utilize World Models to solve the challenge of long-horizon trajectory prediction in open seas.
方法: Method: 提出结合宏动作码本、残差建模与向量化车道先验的混合架构，构建包含矢量车道先验的海事轨迹预测数据集。Proposing a hybrid architecture combining macro-action codebooks, residual modeling, and vectorized lane priors, and constructing a maritime trajectory prediction dataset incorporating vectorized lane priors.
推进: Advance: 在长视界预测任务上显著超越LSTM和Transformer基线，证明了矢量先验与生成式模型结合在复杂动态环境中的有效性。Significantly outperforming LSTM and Transformer baselines on long-horizon prediction tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining vector priors with generative models in complex dynamic environments.
2026-07
Anticipate Before Acting: Future-State-Conditioned Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 指出当前端到端视觉语言导航（VLN）过度依赖动作模仿，缺乏对长程未来状态的显式推理与预判能力。Pointing out that current end-to-end Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) overly relies on action imitation, lacking explicit reasoning and prediction capabilities for long-term future states.
方法: Method: 在Transformer中引入两个可学习Token分别编码“预知未来”与“执行当下”，训练时对齐远端专家轨迹，推理时仅保留预测Token作为前缀。Introducing two learnable Tokens in the Transformer to encode 'predicting the future' and 'executing the present', aligning with distant expert trajectories during training, and retaining only the prediction Token as a prefix during inference.
推进: Advance: 以低成本实现了长程导航性能提升，验证了隐式未来状态条件化对提升策略鲁棒性的作用，但尚未达到完全自监督的生成式想象水平。Achieving long-horizon navigation performance improvements at low cost, validating the role of implicit future state conditioning in enhancing policy robustness, though not yet reaching fully self-supervised generative imagination levels.
2026-07
Predictive Training with Latent Imagination for Visual Quadruped Navigation
动机: Motivation: 为解决四足机器人在动态环境中实时导航的算力瓶颈，试图在训练阶段利用世界模型进行“做梦”以获取未来状态信号。Attempting to overcome the computational bottleneck for real-time navigation of quadruped robots in dynamic environments by utilizing World Models for 'dreaming' during the training phase to obtain future state signals.
方法: Method: 采用JEPA架构结合SIGReg防止表示坍塌，训练时利用预测模块生成未来状态指导策略，推理时切换为无预测的纯反应式策略。Adopting a JEPA architecture combined with SIGReg to prevent representational collapse, using the prediction module to generate future states for guiding the policy during training, and switching to a pure reactive strategy without prediction during inference.
推进: Advance: 实现了训练与推理的解耦，在边缘设备上显著降低延迟，证明了隐式预测信号能有效提升动态避障性能，但泛化性仍受限于训练分布。Achieving decoupling of training and inference, significantly reducing latency on edge devices, and demonstrating that implicit prediction signals can effectively enhance dynamic obstacle avoidance performance, although generalization remains limited by the training distribution.
2026-07
Embodied GPT-5.1: Evidence of a World Model?
动机: Motivation: 挑战传统具身智能中“身体是智能前提”的认知，探索大语言模型在无显式物理训练下是否具备内在的世界模型能力。Challenging the traditional cognition in embodied intelligence that 'the body is a prerequisite for intelligence,' this work explores whether large language models possess intrinsic world model capabilities without explicit physical training.
方法: Method: 利用GPT-5.1进行端到端的空间推理与物理交互测试，通过观察目标移出视野后的位置推断及碰撞后的行为反馈来验证其隐式物理常识。Utilizing GPT-5.1 for end-to-end spatial reasoning and physical interaction testing, it verifies implicit physical common sense by inferring target positions after they move out of the field of view and analyzing behavioral feedback following collisions.
推进: Advance: 揭示了大模型在概率拟合层面具备初步的物理连续性直觉，尽管存在路径规划不优雅和幻觉问题，但为理解纯文本模型向具身世界模型转化的边界提供了新视角。It reveals that large models possess preliminary physical continuity intuition at the probability fitting level; despite issues with ungraceful path planning and hallucinations, it provides a new perspective on the boundaries for transforming pure text models into embodied world models.
2026-07
Genie Sim PanoWorld: An Infinite Indoor 3D World Generation Pipeline via Panoramic Scene Modeling and Simulation
动机: Motivation: 现有具身导航仿真资产生成依赖多视图采集或高算力服务器，难以快速构建大规模、可漫游的室内环境以支持长程规划训练。Existing embodied navigation simulation asset generation relies on multi-view acquisition or high-compute servers, making it difficult to rapidly construct large-scale, explorable indoor environments to support long-horizon planning training.
方法: Method: 提出基于全景图的端到端生成流程，利用单张全景输入在4步采样内直接构建可漫游的无限3D世界，无需显式优化或外部几何先验。Proposes an end-to-end generation process based on panoramic images, utilizing a single panoramic input to directly construct an explorable infinite 3D world within 4 sampling steps, without explicit optimization or external geometric priors.
推进: Advance: 突破了传统NeRF等方法对多视图数据的依赖，实现了单卡运行的高效场景重建，为具身智能提供了低成本、高泛化的快速原型生成器。Breaks through the dependency of traditional NeRF methods on multi-view data, achieving efficient scene reconstruction on a single card, providing a low-cost, high-generalization rapid prototyping generator for embodied intelligence.
2026-07
X-NavDP: Generalizing Navigation Diffusion Policy to Novel Behavior and Embodiments with Group Q-score Reweighted Matching
动机: Motivation: 针对现有扩散策略导航模型在遭遇死胡同或更换机器人本体时泛化能力不足的问题，旨在通过引入强化学习机制弥补纯模仿学习的盲区。Aiming to address the insufficient generalization capability of existing diffusion policy navigation models when encountering dead ends or changing robot embodiments, this work seeks to fill the blind spots of pure imitation learning by introducing reinforcement learning mechanisms.
方法: Method: 提出GQRM框架，将强化学习作为后训练阶段引入预训练的Diffusion Policy，利用RL微调优化导航策略以应对新行为和新本体。Proposing the GQRM framework, which introduces reinforcement learning as a post-training stage into pre-trained Diffusion Policy, utilizing RL fine-tuning to optimize navigation strategies for adapting to new behaviors and new embodiments.
推进: Advance: 成功将仿真环境下的导航成功率从61%提升至84%，真机部署成功率从10%大幅提升至65%，证明了RL后训练能有效解决长尾场景下的决策失效问题。Successfully increasing navigation success rates from 61% to 84% in simulation environments and significantly boosting real-robot deployment success rates from 10% to 65%, demonstrating that RL post-training effectively resolves decision failures in long-tail scenarios.
2026-07
Grounded world models in biological organisms and future embodied AI
动机: Motivation: 批判当前VLA模型过度依赖语言脚手架而缺乏生物体“身体互动构建世界模型”的内驱力，指出被动学习范式的缺陷。Critiquing current VLA models for their over-reliance on linguistic scaffolding and lack of the intrinsic drive of biological organisms to 'construct world models through bodily interaction,' pointing out the defects of passive learning paradigms.
方法: Method: 通过海马体导航、前馈抑制等五个神经回路案例，从生物学角度重构具身智能的理论基础，强调身体与环境的互动优先于语言。Reconstructing the theoretical foundation of embodied intelligence from a biological perspective through five neural circuit cases, such as hippocampal navigation and feedforward inhibition, emphasizing that body-environment interaction takes precedence over language.
推进: Advance: 揭示了当前AI缺乏自我意识与内驱力的本质原因，为未来世界模型从“预测工具”转向“具身认知”提供了理论指引。Revealing the root causes of the current lack of self-awareness and intrinsic drive in AI, providing theoretical guidance for the future transition of World Models from 'prediction tools' to 'embodied cognition.'
2026-07
GPOcc++: Unified Sparse Gaussian Occupancy Prediction with Visual Geometry Priors
动机: Motivation: 试图解决视觉几何先验（表面信息）到体素占据预测（体积信息）的鸿沟，以支持更精确的导航规划。Attempting to bridge the gap between visual geometric priors (surface information) and voxel occupancy prediction (volume information) to support more precise navigation planning.
方法: Method: 提出GPOcc++，利用稀疏高斯表示法结合射线采样策略，将表面点云强制拉伸并融合为体素占据预测。Proposing GPOcc++, which utilizes sparse Gaussian representation combined with ray sampling strategies to forcibly stretch and fuse surface point clouds into voxel occupancy predictions.
推进: Advance: 在保持计算效率的同时提升了体素预测精度，验证了表面到体积转换在具身感知中的有效性，但揭示了其对深度先验质量的强依赖性。Improving voxel prediction accuracy while maintaining computational efficiency, validating the effectiveness of surface-to-volume conversion in embodied perception, but revealing its strong dependence on the quality of depth priors.
2026-07
Ego-Dynamics-Augmented World Model for Autonomous Driving with Zero-Shot Cross-Chassis Adaptation
动机: Motivation: 解决传统BEV世界模型需从像素中隐式解算自运动导致算力浪费及场景建模缩水的问题。Addressing the issue of computational waste and degraded scene modeling caused by traditional BEV World Models implicitly solving ego-motion from pixels.
方法: Method: 提出Ego-Dynamics-Augmented World Model，直接将底盘动力学参数作为先验输入Transformer，显式分离自运动与世界运动。Proposing the Ego-Dynamics-Augmented World Model, which directly inputs chassis dynamics parameters as priors into the Transformer to explicitly separate ego-motion from world motion.
推进: Advance: 实现了零样本跨底盘适应，显著降低了模型对自运动解算的依赖，为物理先验辅助世界模型在自动驾驶中的应用开辟了新路径。Achieving zero-shot cross-chassis adaptation, significantly reducing the model's dependence on ego-motion solving, and opening a new path for the application of physics-prior-assisted World Models in autonomous driving.
2026-07
Learning Safe Agent Behaviour from Human Preferences and Justifications via World Models
动机: Motivation: 旨在通过“做梦”（世界模型模拟）替代昂贵的实地试错，解决安全关键任务中人类偏好与安全理由的对齐难题。Aiming to replace expensive real-world trial-and-error with 'dreaming' (World Model simulation) to solve the alignment problem between human preferences and safety rationales in safety-critical tasks.

Planner-Conditioned Diffusion for Coordinated Multi-Agent Exploration
动机: Motivation: 解决多智能体探索中端到端联合训练困难及行为多样性不足的问题，试图在保持覆盖效率的同时实现多样化的规划风格。Addressing the difficulties of end-to-end joint training and insufficient behavioral diversity in multi-agent exploration, aiming to achieve diverse planning styles while maintaining coverage efficiency.
方法: Method: 设计Planner-Conditioned Diffusion架构，通过条件注入让单一扩散模型模仿多种规划器风格，并配合后处理重排序策略优化群体路径。Designing a Planner-Conditioned Diffusion architecture that uses conditional injection to enable a single diffusion model to mimic multiple planner styles, coupled with a post-processing re-ranking strategy to optimize group paths.
推进: Advance: 展示了通过条件控制而非重新训练即可实现多风格规划的能力，但揭示了当前“各自为政”后的重排序机制在大规模集群下存在计算瓶颈与深度协同缺失的局限。Demonstrating the capability to achieve multi-style planning through conditional control rather than retraining, while revealing limitations of the current 'siloed' re-ranking mechanism in large-scale clusters, including computational bottlenecks and a lack of deep collaboration.

DA-WAM: Decision-Aligned Future Latents for Driving World Models
动机: Motivation: 现有世界模型常将未来预测与决策规划割裂，导致候选轨迹共享同一未来潜变量，无法准确评估特定动作的因果后果。Existing world models often separate future prediction from decision planning, causing candidate trajectories to share the same future latent variable, which prevents accurate assessment of the causal consequences of specific actions.
方法: Method: 提出决策对齐未来潜变量（Decision-Aligned Future Latents），将每个候选轨迹与其独有的未来潜变量绑定，并引入LoRA微调与EMA目标机制。Proposed Decision-Aligned Future Latents, binding each candidate trajectory to its unique future latent variable, and introducing LoRA fine-tuning and EMA objective mechanisms.
推进: Advance: 实现了预测与决策的端到端对齐，在NAVSIM数据集上达到SOTA，验证了“预测即决策”在特定轨迹评估中的有效性。Achieved end-to-end alignment between prediction and decision, reaching SOTA on the NAVSIM dataset, validating the effectiveness of 'prediction as decision' in specific trajectory evaluation.

Reinforced Planning with Latent World Models
动机: Motivation: 单纯具备预测能力的世界模型若缺乏对规划策略本身的优化能力，面对幻觉时极易失效，且传统搜索算法效率低下。World models possessing only prediction capabilities, if lacking optimization ability for the planning strategy itself, are prone to failure when facing hallucinations, and traditional search algorithms are inefficient.
方法: Method: 提出强化规划（Reinforced Planning），将规划过程本身作为可学习策略，利用世界模型进行少量推演替代暴力搜索，并结合Dyna微调抑制幻觉。Proposed Reinforced Planning, treating the planning process itself as a learnable strategy, utilizing the world model for a few rollouts to replace brute-force search, and combining Dyna fine-tuning to suppress hallucinations.
推进: Advance: 将规划从黑盒规则转化为可学习策略，以极少的世界模型推演次数实现了远超传统算法的效率，证明了规划策略可被世界模型直接优化。Transformed planning from black-box rules into a learnable strategy, achieving far superior efficiency over traditional algorithms with minimal world model rollouts, proving that planning strategies can be directly optimized by world models.

Progressive Experience Fusion for Multi-Task World Model Control in Endovascular Navigation
动机: Motivation: 血管内导航等多任务场景下，单一任务经验难以泛化，且传统方法难以在复杂动态环境中平衡多任务控制与计算开销。In multi-task scenarios such as intravascular navigation, single-task experience is difficult to generalize, and traditional methods struggle to balance multi-task control with computational overhead in complex dynamic environments.
方法: Method: 设计渐进式经验融合（PEF）框架，将单任务经验层层打包融合以训练通才控制器，并引入自适应视界机制优化残差距离。Designed the Progressive Experience Fusion (PEF) framework, layering and fusing single-task experiences to train a generalist controller, and introducing an adaptive horizon mechanism to optimize residual distance.
推进: Advance: 成功将世界模型从单一预测工具扩展为多任务通用控制器，在血管导航PoC中展示了优于SAC和TD-MPC2的泛化能力。Successfully extended the world model from a single prediction tool to a multi-task generalist controller, demonstrating superior generalization capabilities over SAC and TD-MPC2 in the intravascular navigation PoC.

World-Model-Grounded LLM Planning for AUV and ASV Navigation Near Offshore Wind Farms
动机: Motivation: 针对大语言模型缺乏物理常识导致在海上风电场等复杂场景中易发生碰撞或逻辑错误的问题，旨在解决LLM无法准确预测推土机动作时长及物理约束的痛点。Aiming to address the issue where large language models lack physical common sense, leading to collisions or logical errors in complex scenarios such as offshore wind farms, this work targets the pain point of LLMs' inability to accurately predict bulldozer action durations and physical constraints.
方法: Method: 构建混合世界模型，底层采用物理公式确保基础约束，神经网络负责补全复杂动态，将LLM的决策层与世界模型的执行层解耦，实现“做什么”与“做多久”的协同。Constructing a hybrid world model where physical formulas at the bottom layer ensure basic constraints, while neural networks complete complex dynamics; this decouples the LLM's decision layer from the world model's execution layer to achieve synergy between 'what to do' and 'how long to do it'.
推进: Advance: 实现了AUV与ASV双平台在仿真环境中的零碰撞验证，并创新性地利用卫星图辅助ASV导航以替代昂贵传感器，证明了混合架构在长尾物理场景下的可行性。Achieved zero-collision verification for AUV and ASV dual platforms in simulation environments, and innovatively utilized satellite imagery to assist ASV navigation as a substitute for expensive sensors, proving the feasibility of the hybrid architecture in long-tail physical scenarios.

Do Better Imagined Rollouts Mean Better Robot Control? A Controlled Study of World-Model Evaluation Under Feedback
动机: Motivation: 质疑当前世界模型研究中“预测误差低”等同于“控制性能好”的假设，揭示离线评估指标与闭环实际表现脱节的现象。Questions the assumption in current world model research that 'low prediction error' equates to 'good control performance', revealing the disconnect between offline evaluation metrics and closed-loop actual performance.
方法: Method: 通过受控实验对比不同世界模型评估协议，分析轨迹回放误差与无测量 Rollout 误差对差速机器人闭环控制的影响。Conducts controlled experiments comparing different world model evaluation protocols to analyze the impact of trajectory replay errors and unmeasured rollout errors on closed-loop control of differential drive robots.
推进: Advance: 发现传统轨迹回放 RMSE 比高级无测量 Rollout 更能预测闭环性能，警示社区需建立模拟真实反馈循环的评估协议而非仅关注预测精度。Finds that traditional trajectory replay RMSE is a better predictor of closed-loop performance than advanced unmeasured rollout errors, warning the community to establish evaluation protocols that simulate real feedback loops rather than focusing solely on prediction accuracy.

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决传统3D地图构建依赖繁琐人工建模且缺乏物理一致性的问题，探索利用生成式视频模型直接构建可导航的3D环境。Aiming to solve the problem that traditional 3D map construction relies on tedious manual modeling and lacks physical consistency, exploring the use of generative video models to directly construct navigable 3D environments.

推进: Advance: 首次实现了从单图到可玩3D地图的零样本生成，成功 bridging 了视频生成与3D几何构建，但受限于SLAM漂移导致物理一致性尚待验证。Achieves zero-shot generation from a single image to a playable 3D map for the first time, successfully bridging video generation and 3D geometric construction, but remains limited by SLAM drift, leaving physical consistency yet to be verified.

方法: Method: 提出MM-Tokens隐式表示架构，通过多模式联合世界与动作建模，在NAVSIM基准上生成64个潜在假设轨迹以评估规划质量。Proposing the MM-Tokens implicit representation architecture, which employs multi-modal joint world and action modeling to generate 64 latent hypothetical trajectories on the NAVSIM benchmark to evaluate planning quality.
推进: Advance: 实现了94.0 PDMS的SOTA性能，证明了多模态联合建模在复杂动态场景下的规划鲁棒性，但揭示了隐式表示在事故归因与实时推理延迟上的工程瓶颈。Achieving a state-of-the-art (SOTA) PDMS performance of 94.0, demonstrating the planning robustness of multi-modal joint modeling in complex dynamic scenarios, while revealing engineering bottlenecks regarding implicit representation in accident attribution and real-time inference latency.

Feeling Terrain Before Crossing: World Models for Off-Road Navigation
动机: Motivation: 解决传统视觉主导的世界模型（如NWM）在野外非结构化地形中因缺乏本体感知（Proprioception）而导致的“视而不见”与翻车风险。Addressing the 'seeing but not perceiving' phenomenon and rollover risks in unstructured outdoor terrains caused by the lack of proprioception in traditional vision-dominated world models (e.g., NWM).
方法: Method: 构建Feel-WM框架，将IMU与编码器数据强制融合进世界模型，使模型能够预测机器人在跨越地形时的物理“体感”而非仅视觉外观。Constructing the Feel-WM framework, which forces the fusion of IMU and encoder data into the world model, enabling the model to predict the robot's physical 'sensation' when traversing terrain rather than merely visual appearance.
推进: Advance: 显著降低了野外导航的翻车率，证实了引入本体感知对于提升非结构化环境泛化性的关键作用，但也暴露了对特定传感器标签依赖及Token压缩带来的精度权衡问题。Significantly reducing rollover rates in outdoor navigation, confirming the critical role of proprioception in enhancing generalization in unstructured environments, while also exposing trade-offs in accuracy due to dependency on specific sensor labels and token compression.

动机: Motivation: 针对社交导航中在线微调扩散策略易破坏原有避障能力（微调焦虑）的痛点，旨在解决真实动态环境中策略适应性与安全性的冲突。Aiming to address the conflict between strategy adaptability and safety in real dynamic environments, this work targets the pain point of online fine-tuning diffusion policies in social navigation, which easily undermines existing obstacle avoidance capabilities (fine-tuning anxiety).
方法: Method: 提出扩散策略重定向（Diffusion Steering）方法，在固定扩散策略的潜空间内通过强化学习（DSRL）调整噪声分布而非直接修改权重。Proposes a Diffusion Steering method that adjusts the noise distribution via Reinforcement Learning (DSRL) within the latent space of a fixed diffusion policy, rather than directly modifying weights.
推进: Advance: 实现了在不牺牲基础避障性能前提下的社交礼仪在线适应，相比残差RL和DPPO显著提升了策略在动态人群中的鲁棒性。Achieves online adaptation of social etiquette without compromising basic obstacle avoidance performance, significantly improving strategy robustness in dynamic crowds compared to Residual RL and DPPO.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有世界模型在具身导航中缺乏形式化安全验证且易产生不可控噪声的问题，旨在将生成过程从随机黑盒转化为可验证的物理约束系统。Aiming to address the lack of formal safety verification in existing world models for embodied navigation and their susceptibility to uncontrollable noise, this work seeks to transform the generation process from a stochastic black box into a verifiable physical constraint system.
方法: Method: 提出随机世界模型，将生成噪声显式绑定到太阳高度角等物理量，利用符号分析与卷积头结合的方式对视觉反馈系统进行灰度与RGB验证。Proposing a stochastic world model that explicitly binds generation noise to physical quantities such as solar elevation angle, utilizing a combination of symbolic analysis and convolutional heads to verify the visual feedback system in both grayscale and RGB.
推进: Advance: 首次将世界模型从单纯的预测工具提升为可形式化验证的安全组件，显著降低了参数规模并证明了物理量化在缓解验证难题上的有效性。For the first time elevating world models from mere prediction tools to formally verifiable safety components, significantly reducing parameter scale and demonstrating the effectiveness of physical quantification in alleviating verification challenges.

PhysWAM: Physically Consistent World Action Model for Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 为解决现有生成式规划模型缺乏真实物理几何约束、易产生违背物理规律轨迹的问题，试图赋予世界模型真正的物理一致性。To address the lack of true physical geometric constraints in existing generative planning models and their tendency to generate trajectories violating physical laws, this work attempts to endow world models with genuine physical consistency.
方法: Method: 设计耦合点投影（CPP）机制，强制将生成的深度与运动投影至3D空间并与LiDAR真值对齐，同时采用多轨迹采样中位数策略进行决策。Designing a Coupled Point Projection (CPP) mechanism that forces generated depth and motion to be projected into 3D space and aligned with LiDAR ground truth, while employing a median strategy across multiple trajectory samples for decision-making.
推进: Advance: 通过引入几何硬约束显著提升了规划方案的安全性与物理合理性，验证了物理一致性对降低仿真与实车部署鸿沟的关键作用。Significantly enhancing the safety and physical rationality of planning schemes by introducing geometric hard constraints, validating the critical role of physical consistency in bridging the gap between simulation and real-world deployment.

Optimal Transport Meets Reinforcement Learning: A Survey
动机: Motivation: 针对传统散度在分布不重叠时失效的痛点，旨在利用最优传输的几何成本特性解决世界模型生成分布与真实物理分布之间的对齐难题。Aiming to address the pain point where traditional divergences fail when distributions do not overlap, this work seeks to leverage the geometric cost characteristics of Optimal Transport to solve the alignment problem between the distribution generated by world models and the true physical distribution.
方法: Method: 系统综述了将最优传输理论引入强化学习框架的策略，包括作为奖励信号、正则项及信任域约束，以优化生成式规划中的分布匹配。This paper systematically reviews strategies for introducing Optimal Transport theory into reinforcement learning frameworks, including as reward signals, regularization terms, and trust region constraints, to optimize distribution matching in generative planning.
推进: Advance: 揭示了OT在解决长尾场景下分布不重叠时的梯度有效性，为基于世界模型的导航规划提供了更稳健的分布优化理论支撑。It reveals the gradient effectiveness of OT in resolving distribution non-overlap in long-tail scenarios, providing more robust theoretical support for distribution optimization in world model-based navigation planning.
2026-10
Cross-entropy optimization with prioritized constraints
动机: Motivation: 为了解决基于世界模型的规划中，因模型预测误差导致的安全约束失效问题，提出一种不依赖复杂权重调优的优先级约束优化机制。To address the issue of safety constraint failure in world model-based planning caused by model prediction errors, a priority-constrained optimization mechanism is proposed that does not rely on complex weight tuning.
方法: Method: 提出TierCEM方法，通过引入优先级过滤器，在交叉熵优化中强制高优先级约束（如避障）优先满足，允许低优先级目标在冲突时妥协。The TierCEM method is proposed, which introduces a priority filter to enforce the satisfaction of high-priority constraints (such as obstacle avoidance) first in cross-entropy optimization, allowing low-priority goals to compromise in case of conflicts.
推进: Advance: 将世界模型的预测不确定性转化为可管理的约束层级，在工程层面提升了生成式规划在动态环境中的实时安全鲁棒性。By transforming the prediction uncertainty of world models into manageable constraint hierarchies, the real-time safety robustness of generative planning in dynamic environments is enhanced at the engineering level.
2026-10
H-JEPA: End-to-End Learning of Hierarchical World Models for Visual Planning
动机: Motivation: 针对现有世界模型在长视界规划中因过度关注不可预测的细碎像素细节而导致效率低下的问题，旨在通过分层架构实现状态抽象。Aiming to address the inefficiency of existing World Models in long-horizon planning caused by excessive focus on unpredictable fine-grained pixel details, this work seeks to achieve state abstraction via a hierarchical architecture.
方法: Method: 提出H-JEPA架构，利用高层网络过滤不可预测的细碎信息，仅保留慢速且关键的状态变量，并结合逆动力学进行端到端训练。Proposing the H-JEPA architecture, which utilizes a high-level network to filter out unpredictable fine-grained information, retaining only slow-moving and critical state variables, and combining this with inverse dynamics for end-to-end training.
推进: Advance: 成功将分层预测从理论转化为可训练的端到端系统，在长视界任务（AntMaze）中成功率显著提升，验证了“选择性遗忘”对规划的有效性。Successfully transforming hierarchical prediction from theory into a trainable end-to-end system, significantly improving success rates in long-horizon tasks (AntMaze), thereby validating the effectiveness of 'selective forgetting' for planning.

Ultrasound Operator Guidance Using World Modeling and Retrieval Based Action Planning
动机: Motivation: 为解决复杂物理场景下参数化模型拟合困难及数据稀缺问题，试图利用人类专家的操作经验直接构建非参数化世界模型。Aiming to address the difficulties in fitting parametric models and data scarcity in complex physical scenarios, this work attempts to construct a non-parametric World Model directly using human expert operational experience.
方法: Method: 摒弃传统参数拟合，采用基于检索的多步规划策略，将专家操作录像库作为世界模型，通过检索相似历史轨迹指导当前导航决策。Abandoning traditional parameter fitting, it adopts a retrieval-based multi-step planning strategy, treating a library of expert operation videos as a World Model to guide current navigation decisions by retrieving similar historical trajectories.
推进: Advance: 在颈动脉超声等高难度导航任务中将成功率从50%提升至86%，证明了在特定领域“检索即模型”的经验主义方法优于理论拟合。Increasing success rates from 50% to 86% in high-difficulty navigation tasks such as carotid ultrasound, demonstrating that the empiricism of 'retrieval as model' outperforms theoretical fitting in specific domains.

EpicWorldModel: Exploration-driven Planning with Latent World Models
动机: Motivation: 针对传统确定性世界模型在遮挡等视觉受限场景下容易产生幻觉（一本正经胡说八道）的缺陷，旨在引入不确定性以增强探索能力。Aiming to introduce uncertainty to enhance exploration capabilities, addressing the defect of traditional deterministic World Models generating hallucinations (confidently stating nonsense) in visually constrained scenarios like occlusion.
方法: Method: 利用Flow Matching技术替代单点回归，使模型能够生成多种可能的未来轨迹，从而在潜在空间中显式建模不确定性以辅助规划。Utilizing Flow Matching technology to replace single-point regression, enabling the model to generate multiple possible future trajectories, thereby explicitly modeling uncertainty in latent space to assist planning.
推进: Advance: 在视觉受限的迷宫导航任务中比确定性模型提升22%，有效缓解了遮挡导致的规划失效，但同时也揭示了高计算成本对实时控制的挑战。Improving performance by 22% over deterministic models in maze navigation tasks with visual constraints, effectively mitigating planning failures caused by occlusion, while also revealing the challenge of high computational costs for real-time control.

SearchWorld: Spatial Value-Grounded Imagination for UAV Object Search via World Models
动机: Motivation: 针对无人机城市搜索中视野受限导致的“盲人摸象”难题，现有方案要么缺乏前瞻性的显式地图，要么因图像生成模型推理延迟高而难以进行有效的空间规划。Addressing the 'blind men touching an elephant' challenge caused by limited field of view in UAV urban search, existing solutions either lack proactive explicit maps or struggle with effective spatial planning due to high inference latency in image generation models.
方法: Method: 提出一种显式空间记忆与隐式想象相结合的架构，利用BEV网格存储探索状态与障碍物，并解码价值层来指导决策，而非单纯学习标量Critic。Proposing an architecture combining explicit spatial memory with implicit imagination, utilizing BEV grids to store exploration states and obstacles, and decoding value layers to guide decision-making rather than solely learning a scalar Critic.
推进: Advance: 实现了将世界模型从被动预测转化为主动的空间价值引导，显著提升了在复杂受限视野下的长程规划能力，为无人机自主搜索提供了新的范式。Achieving the transformation of world models from passive prediction to active spatial value guidance, significantly enhancing long-horizon planning capabilities under complex limited fields of view and providing a new paradigm for UAV autonomous search.

VLA 端到端导航与控制VLA End-to-End Navigation and Control

VLA 端到端导航旨在打破感知与控制的割裂，利用大模型直接映射视觉语言指令至连续动作。早期工作如 NaVid 尝试极简输入，但受限于推理延迟；随后 NaVILA 和 Uni-NaVid 引入分层架构与视频压缩技术，平衡了泛化与实时性。自动驾驶领域（Orion, AutoVLA）进一步探索语义规划与物理轨迹的对齐，通过“快慢思考”或扩散模型解决 VLM 动作离散化与幻觉问题。最新研究（Lagrange, Green for Go）转向能量场约束与语义分割，试图在保持开放词汇泛化能力的同时，确保运动学约束与长尾场景的安全性，推动 VLA 从仿真走向真实部署。VLA end-to-end navigation aims to break the disconnect between perception and control, leveraging large models to directly map visual-language instructions to continuous actions. Early works like NaVid attempted minimal inputs but were limited by inference latency; subsequently, NaVILA and Uni-NaVid introduced hierarchical architectures and video compression techniques to balance generalization and real-time performance. In the autonomous driving domain (Orion, AutoVLA), further exploration of aligning semantic planning with physical trajectories has been conducted, addressing VLM action discretization and hallucination issues through 'fast-slow thinking' or diffusion models. Latest research (Lagrange, Green for Go) shifts towards energy field constraints and semantic segmentation, attempting to ensure kinematic constraints and safety in long-tail scenarios while maintaining open-vocabulary generalization capabilities, thereby driving VLA from simulation to real-world deployment.

推进: Advance: 在仿真中达到 SOTA 并成功迁移至真机，证明了纯视觉 VLM 具备填平 Sim2Real 鸿沟的潜力。Achieves SOTA in simulation and successfully transfers to real hardware, demonstrating the potential of pure visual VLMs to bridge the Sim2Real gap.

Embodied GPT-5.1: Evidence of a World Model?
动机: Motivation: 挑战具身智能必须依赖物理训练的传统认知，探索大模型是否仅凭概率拟合即可涌现出基础的世界模型与空间推理能力。Challenging the traditional cognition that embodied intelligence must rely on physical training, this work explores whether large models can only rely on probability fitting to elicit basic world model and spatial reasoning capabilities.
方法: Method: 在无需显式物理引擎训练的情况下，直接利用 GPT-5.1 处理视觉语言指令，通过自回归生成机制预测连续动作序列。Without explicit physical engine training, it directly utilizes GPT-5.1 to process vision-language instructions, predicting continuous action sequences through autoregressive generation mechanisms.
推进: Advance: 揭示了大模型在缺乏物理约束时仍能进行目标位置推断与碰撞后行为修正，但也暴露了其在长尾场景中易产生路径螺旋化与幻觉的脆弱性。It reveals that large models can still perform target position inference and post-collision behavioral correction even without physical constraints, but also exposes their vulnerability to path spiraling and hallucinations in long-tail scenarios.

HumanCLAW: Can Vision-Language Models Act Through a Body?
动机: Motivation: 旨在测试VLM是否具备通过物理身体执行动作的具身智能，揭示当前模型缺乏“具身自我意识”及在真实物理约束下决策的缺陷。Aims to test whether VLMs possess embodied intelligence capable of executing actions through a physical body, revealing current models' lack of 'embodied self-awareness' and deficiencies in decision-making under real physical constraints.
方法: Method: 采用决策与执行解耦架构，让VLM仅输出原子技能指令，由物理引擎模拟碰撞与摔倒等物理反馈，构建半物理仿真环境进行零样本测试。Adopts a decoupled architecture of decision and execution, allowing VLMs to output only atomic skill instructions, while a physics engine simulates physical feedback such as collisions and falls, constructing a semi-physical simulation environment for zero-shot testing.
推进: Advance: 通过低通过率（16.8%）量化了VLM在缺乏物理本体感知时的行动能力瓶颈，证明了单纯依靠语言模型无法解决连续控制中的平衡与避障问题。Quantifies the action capability bottleneck of VLMs lacking physical body perception through a low pass rate (16.8%), proving that relying solely on language models cannot solve balance and obstacle avoidance issues in continuous control.

TEA-AgriVLN: Traversability Estimation Alarm for Agricultural Vision-and-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 针对农业非结构化场景中传统 VLA 导航因忽视地形可通行性而导致的失败问题，旨在引入安全机制以弥补单纯依赖语言指令的不足。Aiming to address the failure of traditional VLA navigation in agricultural unstructured scenarios caused by neglecting terrain traversability, this work seeks to introduce safety mechanisms to compensate for the insufficiencies of relying solely on language instructions.
方法: Method: 提出 TEA 模块，利用 VLM 结合 SAM 对视觉输入进行可通行性评估，作为‘安全哨兵’在检测到危险动作时触发重规划。Proposing the TEA module, which utilizes VLM combined with SAM to assess visual input for traversability, acting as a 'safety sentinel' to trigger re-planning upon detecting dangerous actions.
推进: Advance: 在 AgriVLN 基准上将成功率从 0.47 提升至 0.54，证明了在复杂植被环境下引入显式地形感知约束对提升导航鲁棒性的必要性。Improving success rates from 0.47 to 0.54 on the AgriVLN benchmark, proving the necessity of introducing explicit terrain perception constraints to enhance navigation robustness in complex vegetation environments.

Discriminative Barrier Functions for Safe Adversarial Imitation Learning from Observation
动机: Motivation: 针对端到端模仿学习中专家演示缺乏显式避障指令导致的安全隐患，解决策略在未见障碍物前易发生碰撞的问题。Addressing safety hazards caused by the lack of explicit obstacle avoidance instructions in expert demonstrations in end-to-end imitation learning, solving the problem of strategies easily colliding before encountering unseen obstacles.
方法: Method: 将控制屏障函数（CBF）作为硬约束嵌入对抗模仿学习的判别器奖励搜索空间，构建判别性屏障函数（DBF）以强制策略满足安全边界。Embedding Control Barrier Functions (CBF) as hard constraints into the discriminator reward search space of adversarial imitation learning to construct Discriminative Barrier Functions (DBF) to force strategies to satisfy safety boundaries.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需显式避障训练数据即可泛化至未见障碍物场景的安全导航，但揭示了纯模仿学习在复杂物理约束下的实机部署成功率瓶颈。Achieving safe navigation generalization to unseen obstacle scenarios without explicit obstacle avoidance training data, but revealing the bottleneck of real-robot deployment success rates for pure imitation learning under complex physical constraints.

Joint On-and-Off Policy Learning for Vision-and-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决视觉语言导航（VLN）中模仿学习与强化学习割裂的问题，以平衡高泛化能力与策略训练的稳定性。Aims to address the fragmentation between imitation learning and reinforcement learning in visual language navigation (VLN), balancing high generalization capability with strategy training stability.
方法: Method: 提出联合在线与离线策略学习框架（JOP-VLN），通过高熵采样策略结合DAgger数据收集与GRPO奖励优化，统一多模态指令与动作映射。Proposes a joint online and offline strategy learning framework (JOP-VLN), unifying multi-modal instruction and action mapping through high-entropy sampling strategies combined with DAgger data collection and GRPO reward optimization.
推进: Advance: 在未见场景下显著提升了泛化性能与实机few-shot适应能力，但暴露了复杂多阶段训练管线导致的推理延迟与实时性不足问题。Significantly improves generalization performance and real-world few-shot adaptability in unseen scenarios, but exposes issues of inference latency and insufficient real-time performance caused by complex multi-stage training pipelines.

Distributionally Robust and Safe Imitation Learning
动机: Motivation: 针对模仿学习策略在面对环境不确定性及策略漂移时缺乏多步安全保证的缺陷，试图构建鲁棒的导航策略。Aims to construct robust navigation strategies to address the defect of imitation learning strategies lacking multi-step safety guarantees when facing environmental uncertainty and strategy drift.
方法: Method: 结合泰勒级数模仿学习（TaSIL）与L1-DRAC，通过Wasserstein球优化最坏情况分布，将鲁棒控制理论引入端到端模仿学习框架。Combines Taylor series imitation learning (TaSIL) with L1-DRAC, optimizing worst-case distributions via Wasserstein balls to introduce robust control theory into end-to-end imitation learning frameworks.
推进: Advance: 在无人机实机实验中验证了单步预测下的安全性，但计算复杂度的爆炸式增长限制了其在长序列导航中的实时应用效率。Validates safety under single-step prediction in UAV real-world experiments, but the explosive growth in computational complexity limits its real-time application efficiency in long-sequence navigation.

MAMMOTH: A Multi-Modal End-to-End Policy for Off-Road Mobility Robust to Missing Modality
动机: Motivation: 解决越野导航中因传感器故障或夜间低光照导致模态缺失，进而引发端到端策略失效的鲁棒性难题。Addresses the robustness challenge of end-to-end strategy failure caused by modality loss due to sensor faults or low-light conditions at night in off-road navigation.
方法: Method: 构建多模态端到端策略MAMMOTH，利用稀疏混合专家（MoE）融合RGB、热成像等多源数据，并通过训练时随机模态屏蔽强制模型学习缺失模态下的推理能力。Constructs a multi-modal end-to-end strategy MAMMOTH, utilizing sparse mixture of experts (MoE) to fuse RGB, thermal imaging, and other multi-source data, and forcing the model to learn reasoning capabilities under missing modalities via random modality masking during training.
推进: Advance: 证明了在真实机器人上通过隐式规则注入（如IMU启发式）可显著提升极端环境下的导航鲁棒性，但高硬件依赖限制了其通用泛化潜力。Demonstrates that implicit rule injection (e.g., IMU heuristics) on real robots can significantly enhance navigation robustness in extreme environments, but high hardware dependency limits its generalization potential.

动机: Motivation: 试图在自动驾驶VLA中平衡像素级精细控制与潜在表示的鲁棒性，解决单一世界模型在长尾场景下因果推理失效的问题。Attempts to balance pixel-level fine-grained control with robustness of latent representations in autonomous driving VLA, addressing the failure of causal reasoning in single world models under long-tail scenarios.
方法: Method: 提出混合世界建模架构（HyWorldVLA），结合像素级监督与潜在空间表示，利用VAE结构增强对复杂交通场景的预测能力。Proposes a hybrid world modeling architecture (HyWorldVLA), combining pixel-level supervision with latent space representations and utilizing VAE structures to enhance prediction capabilities for complex traffic scenarios.
推进: Advance: 在NAVSIM数据集上刷新SOTA并提升了工业界落地性，但单目视觉局限与对微小交通信号（如刹车灯）的捕捉不足揭示了当前世界模型在因果推理上的根本短板。Refreshes SOTA on the NAVSIM dataset and improves industrial deployability, but the limitations of monocular vision and insufficient capture of minor traffic signals (e.g., brake lights) reveal fundamental shortcomings in current world models regarding causal reasoning.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有 VLA 端到端导航中视觉语言直接映射动作导致的“黑盒”不可解释性及动态场景下缺乏前瞻规划的问题，旨在引入类脑皮层的分步推理机制。Targeting the issues of 'black box' uninterpretability caused by direct visual-language-to-action mapping in existing end-to-end VLA navigation, and the lack of forward-looking planning in dynamic scenarios, it aims to introduce a brain-cortex-like step-by-step reasoning mechanism.
方法: Method: 提出 DBFly 框架，利用 Qwen3-VL 大模型在飞行前进行显式的空间深思熟虑，结合隐式走廊构建与收敛停止策略，替代直接的感知控制映射。Proposes the DBFly framework, which utilizes the Qwen3-VL large model for explicit spatial deliberation before flight, combined with implicit corridor construction and convergence stopping strategies, to replace direct perception-control mapping.
推进: Advance: 实现了从“直觉式”端到端控制向“理性”分步推理的范式转变，有效解决了传统方法中到达目标后无法精准停止的长尾问题，提升了复杂环境下的决策可解释性。Achieves a paradigm shift from 'intuitive' end-to-end control to 'rational' step-by-step reasoning, effectively solving the long-tail problem of imprecise stopping after reaching the target in traditional methods, and improving decision interpretability in complex environments.

推进: Advance: 证明了恒定减速策略在多项指标上优于复杂的 VLM 控制器，打破了“端到端即智能”的迷思，并明确了 VLM 仅在纵向危险检测上具有有限价值。Proved that constant deceleration strategies outperform complex VLM controllers across multiple metrics, breaking the myth that 'end-to-end equals intelligence', and clarifying that VLMs have only limited value in longitudinal hazard detection.

方法: Method: 构建失败记忆库存储翻车现场，在推理时检索相似路况并动态调用LoRA调整参数，无需复杂的强化学习微调。Constructing a failure memory bank to store rollover scenes, retrieving similar road conditions during inference and dynamically calling LoRA to adjust parameters, without requiring complex reinforcement learning fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 在NAVSIM上刷新SOTA并显著降低PDMS指标，证明了工程化记忆增强能有效提升长尾场景的鲁棒性，尽管引入了26ms的推理延迟。Refreshing SOTA on NAVSIM and significantly reducing PDMS metrics, proving that engineering-enhanced memory can effectively improve robustness in long-tail scenarios, despite introducing a 26ms inference latency.

BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 针对现有VLA模型在融合语义规则与动态预测时，语义信号往往压制预测信号导致决策混乱的问题，旨在解决多模态信号在动作空间中的冲突。Addressing the problem where existing VLA models often experience decision chaos due to semantic signals suppressing prediction signals when fusing semantic rules and dynamic predictions, aiming to resolve conflicts among multimodal signals in the action space.
方法: Method: 提出BrainWAM架构，模仿人脑左右脑分工，分别由VLA处理语义规则、WAM处理预测动力学，并通过胼胝体（CAB）和小脑（CIF）机制在动作空间进行协调握手。Proposing the BrainWAM architecture, which mimics the division of labor between the left and right hemispheres of the human brain, with VLA handling semantic rules and WAM handling predictive dynamics, coordinating and shaking hands in the action space through Corpus Callosum (CAB) and Cerebellum (CIF) mechanisms.
推进: Advance: 在NAVSIM上实现了语义先验与预测动力学的有效解耦与融合，显著提升了自动驾驶在复杂场景下的决策稳定性，为多模态融合提供了新的架构范式。Achieving effective decoupling and fusion of semantic priors and predictive dynamics on NAVSIM, significantly improving decision stability for autonomous driving in complex scenarios and providing a new architectural paradigm for multimodal fusion.

方法: Method: 设计“点穴”策略让VLM输出2D像素点，结合SLAM系统完成3D坐标转换，并引入按需思考机制优化推理效率。Designed a 'Point-Acupuncture' strategy for VLMs to output 2D pixel points, combined with a SLAM system to complete 3D coordinate conversion, and introduced an on-demand thinking mechanism to optimize inference efficiency.

LightNav-0: Eliciting VLM Spatial Intelligence for Generalist Embodied Navigation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有VLA导航模型算力消耗大、模块割裂导致推理延迟高的问题，旨在通过极简架构实现从视觉语言指令到连续动作的端到端映射。Aiming to address the issues of high computational cost and module fragmentation leading to high inference latency in existing VLA navigation models, by achieving end-to-end mapping from visual language instructions to continuous actions through a minimalist architecture.
方法: Method: 提出LightNav-0架构，利用双通道点选机制作为中间推理步骤，并采用残差向量量化（RVQ）将动作压缩为三个Token，以小巧VLM替代复杂分拆模块。Proposing the LightNav-0 architecture, utilizing a dual-channel point-and-click mechanism as an intermediate reasoning step, and employing Residual Vector Quantization (RVQ) to compress actions into three tokens, replacing complex decomposed modules with a compact VLM.
推进: Advance: 在保持精度的同时显著降低了计算负载，验证了将3D空间推理压缩至2D Token序列的可行性，实现了跨四足、人形及无人机平台的零样本泛化。Significantly reducing computational load while maintaining accuracy, validating the feasibility of compressing 3D spatial reasoning into 2D token sequences, and achieving zero-shot generalization across quadruped, humanoid, and drone platforms.

SpikingVLA: Asynchronous Spiking Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 针对现有 VLA 模型在端到端导航中推理延迟高、能耗大且难以满足实时控制需求的问题，旨在通过神经形态计算范式突破传统 ANN 架构的瓶颈。Addressing the issues of high inference latency, significant energy consumption, and difficulty in meeting real-time control requirements of existing VLA models in end-to-end navigation, aiming to break through the bottlenecks of traditional ANN architectures via the neuromorphic computing paradigm.
方法: Method: 提出 SpikingVLA 模型，利用 ANN-to-SNN 转换结合创新的 DIF 神经元机制处理树突混合，并引入异步执行架构以解耦视觉、语言与动作的时序依赖。Proposing the SpikingVLA model, which utilizes ANN-to-SNN conversion combined with an innovative DIF neuron mechanism to handle dendritic mixing, and introduces an asynchronous execution architecture to decouple temporal dependencies among vision, language, and actions.
推进: Advance: 成功将首帧动作时间（TTFA）降低 11 倍，在保持导航精度的同时显著提升了实时性，为 VLA 在资源受限边缘设备上的部署提供了新的异步计算路径。Successfully reducing the Time-to-First-Action (TTFA) by 11 times, significantly improving real-time performance while maintaining navigation accuracy, and providing a new asynchronous computing path for deploying VLA on resource-constrained edge devices.

开放词汇与长尾泛化Open Vocabulary and Long-Tail Generalization

本方向聚焦于解决具身导航中开放词汇指令与长尾场景的泛化难题，核心思路是从依赖固定类别检测转向利用大模型语义理解与因果推理。演进路径上，早期工作通过构建大规模开放词汇数据集（如HM3D-OVON）暴露了端到端模型在未见类别上的脆弱性；随后研究引入外部记忆（语义地图）、共形预测及反事实推理（如OmniDrive、Alpamayo-R1）以增强模型对未知环境的认知与决策鲁棒性；最新进展则尝试将物理约束（拉格朗日力学）与精细化的视觉语言对齐（如See-and-Reach）结合，试图在保持语义泛化能力的同时解决长尾场景下的精确控制与状态表征缺陷，但仍面临仿真到现实迁移及模型幻觉等根本挑战。This direction focuses on solving the generalization challenges of open-vocabulary instructions and long-tail scenarios in embodied navigation. The core idea shifts from relying on fixed-category detection to leveraging large model semantic understanding and causal reasoning. In terms of evolution, early works exposed the fragility of end-to-end models on unseen categories by constructing large-scale open-vocabulary datasets (e.g., HM3D-OVON); subsequent research introduced external memory (semantic maps), conformal prediction, and counterfactual reasoning (e.g., OmniDrive, Alpamayo-R1) to enhance model cognition and decision robustness in unknown environments; latest advances attempt to combine physical constraints (Lagrangian mechanics) with fine-grained visual-language alignment (e.g., See-and-Reach), aiming to solve precise control and state representation defects in long-tail scenarios while maintaining semantic generalization capabilities, yet still facing fundamental challenges such as simulation-to-reality transfer and model hallucination.

动机: Motivation: 针对VLM在具身问答中记忆缺失与幻觉问题，旨在通过外部语义地图与严格验证机制提升探索的可靠性。Addresses VLM memory loss and hallucination issues in embodied QA, aiming to improve exploration reliability through external semantic maps and strict verification mechanisms.

Alpamayo-R1: Bridging Reasoning and Action Prediction for Generalizable Autonomous Driving in the Long Tail
动机: Motivation: 自动驾驶端到端模型在长尾场景下缺乏可解释性与因果推理能力，导致泛化性能不稳定。Autonomous driving end-to-end models lack interpretability and causal reasoning capabilities in long-tail scenarios, leading to unstable generalization performance.
方法: Method: 提出Alpamayo-R1，利用因果链（CoC）将推理与动作预测解耦，并基于因果数据集进行强化学习微调。Proposing Alpamayo-R1, which decouples reasoning from action prediction using a Chain of Causality (CoC) and performs reinforcement learning fine-tuning based on causal datasets.
推进: Advance: 证明了因果推理能显著提升长尾场景下的泛化性，但依赖大规模仿真数据与高成本底座模型，实车落地仍面临物理世界复杂性的挑战。Demonstrating that causal reasoning significantly enhances generalization in long-tail scenarios, yet relying on large-scale simulation data and high-cost foundation models, with real-world deployment still facing challenges from the complexity of the physical world.

Lagrange: An Open-Vocabulary, Energy-Based Sparse Framework for Generalized End-to-End Driving
动机: Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶模型在开放词汇泛化与运动学约束之间存在矛盾，难以兼顾语义理解与连续控制。Existing end-to-end driving models face a contradiction between open-vocabulary generalization and kinematic constraints, making it difficult to balance semantic understanding with continuous control.
方法: Method: 提出Lagrange框架，利用拉格朗日力学将语义信息转化为能量场，在保持VLM泛化能力的同时满足运动学约束。Proposing the Lagrange framework, which utilizes Lagrangian mechanics to transform semantic information into energy fields, satisfying kinematic constraints while maintaining VLM generalization capabilities.

A Topological Representation with Object-Path Graphs for Open-Vocabulary Instance Navigation
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决开放词汇实例导航中语义理解与拓扑路径规划割裂的问题，避免构建高成本的稠密语义地图。Aims to address the disconnection between semantic understanding and topological path planning in open-vocabulary instance navigation, avoiding the construction of high-cost dense semantic maps.
方法: Method: 提出一种融合物体与路径的图结构（Object-Path Graph），将语义节点与拓扑连接统一建模，并通过视觉伺服对齐角差实现节点间的导航。Proposes a graph structure (Object-Path Graph) fusing objects and paths, unifying modeling of semantic nodes and topological connections, and achieving navigation between nodes via visual servoing to align angular differences.
推进: Advance: 实现了语义与拓扑的端到端统一表征，证明了在无需稠密地图的情况下也能完成开放词汇导航，但揭示了单纯依赖视觉特征匹配在纹理单一或光照变化下的鲁棒性缺陷。Achieves an end-to-end unified representation of semantics and topology, proving that open-vocabulary navigation can be completed without dense maps, but reveals robustness defects when relying solely on visual feature matching under single textures or lighting changes.

多模态数据与基准构建Multimodal Data and Benchmark Construction

该方向致力于突破具身导航在数据规模、多模态融合度及物理真实感上的瓶颈。核心思路从单一模态向多模态（视觉、语言、物理）融合演进，利用生成式模型构建高保真、可交互的仿真环境以解决真实数据稀缺问题。工作重心从静态场景评估转向动态终身学习与反事实推理，通过构建开放词汇、大规模对话及复杂物理交互基准，揭示端到端模型在语义泛化、空间记忆及物理理解上的根本缺陷，推动导航智能从“路径规划”向“环境理解与适应”跨越。This direction aims to break through bottlenecks in embodied navigation regarding data scale, multimodal fusion, and physical realism. The core approach evolves from single-modality to multimodal (visual, linguistic, physical) fusion, utilizing generative models to construct high-fidelity, interactive simulation environments to address the scarcity of real data. The focus shifts from static scene evaluation to dynamic lifelong learning and counterfactual reasoning, revealing fundamental defects of end-to-end models in semantic generalization, spatial memory, and physical understanding through the construction of open-vocabulary, large-scale dialogue, and complex physical interaction benchmarks, thereby promoting the leap of navigation intelligence from 'path planning' to 'environment understanding and adaptation'.

GOAT-Bench: A Benchmark for Multi-Modal Lifelong Navigation
动机: Motivation: 现有导航基准过于单一且缺乏长期记忆机制，无法模拟机器人在真实环境中持续学习的需求。Existing navigation benchmarks are overly simplistic and lack long-term memory mechanisms, failing to simulate the continuous learning needs of robots in real environments.
方法: Method: 构建GOAT-Bench，整合68万条开放词汇数据，统一多模态输入与终身学习任务框架。Constructing GOAT-Bench, integrating 680,000 open-vocabulary data samples to unify multimodal input and lifelong learning task frameworks.
推进: Advance: 揭示了端到端强化学习在路径效率上的不足，并证明CLIP特征在实例级导航中泛化能力有限，暴露了当前模型缺乏真正环境记忆的问题。Revealing the deficiencies of end-to-end reinforcement learning in path efficiency and demonstrating that CLIP features have limited generalization capabilities in instance-level navigation, exposing the current lack of true environmental memory in models.

推进: Advance: 证明纯端到端模型在未见类别上失效，必须依赖外挂检测器，深刻揭示了当前模型在语义泛化与深层理解上的根本短板。Proving that pure end-to-end models fail on unseen categories, requiring reliance on external detectors, profoundly revealing the fundamental shortcomings of current models in semantic generalization and deep understanding.

方法: Method: 提出同步驱动的通信门控机制，利用Hindsight gating将强化学习方差问题转化为监督分类，实现早期状态同步。Proposing a synchronous-driven communication gating mechanism, it utilizes Hindsight gating to transform the reinforcement learning variance problem into supervised classification, achieving early state synchronization.

AdvNav: Behavior-Guided Black-Box Adversarial Attacks on Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 现有具身导航基准多依赖白盒梯度信息，缺乏模拟真实世界中无模型访问、仅凭行为反馈进行安全评估的机制，难以揭示模型在物理交互中的脆弱性。Existing embodied navigation benchmarks often rely on white-box gradient information, lacking mechanisms to simulate real-world model-free access and safety evaluation based solely on behavioral feedback, making it difficult to reveal model vulnerabilities in physical interactions.
方法: Method: 提出AdvNav框架，利用行为引导的黑盒攻击策略，通过Perlin噪声生成隐蔽扰动，仅依赖任务完成度反馈而非内部梯度来攻击视觉语言导航模型。The AdvNav framework is proposed, utilizing behavior-guided black-box attack strategies that generate covert perturbations via Perlin noise, attacking Vision-Language Navigation models relying solely on task completion feedback rather than internal gradients.
推进: Advance: 突破了传统白盒攻击对模型内部结构的依赖，验证了生成式扰动在物理世界光照变化下的潜在鲁棒性，为构建高保真、反事实的导航安全基准提供了新范式。It breaks through the traditional dependence of white-box attacks on internal model structures, verifying the potential robustness of generative perturbations under physical world lighting changes, providing a new paradigm for building high-fidelity, counterfactual navigation safety benchmarks.

DaViNCi: A Dataset Towards Outdoor Vision-and-Language Navigation with Continuous Actions and Dynamic Elements
动机: Motivation: 针对户外导航中离散动作与静态场景假设的局限，试图构建能反映真实交通流连续动态特性的基准，以测试模型在复杂开放环境下的适应能力。Addressing the limitations of discrete actions and static scene assumptions in outdoor navigation, attempting to construct a benchmark that reflects the continuous dynamic characteristics of real traffic flow to test model adaptability in complex open environments.
方法: Method: 在CARLA仿真器中构建DaViNCi数据集，包含6k条连续动作轨迹，利用VLM生成指令并结合人工微调，建立从离散图推演至连续轨迹的户外VLN评估体系。Constructing the DaViNCi dataset in the CARLA simulator, containing 6k continuous action trajectories, utilizing VLMs to generate instructions combined with manual fine-tuning to establish an outdoor VLN evaluation system from discrete graph deduction to continuous trajectories.
推进: Advance: 揭示了现有端到端模型在连续动态空间中的“盲人摸象”困境，通过实验证明在连续动作空间下成功率暴跌，指出了当前方法在应对真实世界复杂动态交互时的根本性不足。Revealing the 'blind men touching an elephant' dilemma of existing end-to-end models in continuous dynamic spaces, proving through experiments that success rates plummet under continuous action spaces, pointing out the fundamental insufficiency of current methods in handling complex dynamic interactions in the real world.

DeepInsight II: One Trace from Benchmark to Robot
动机: Motivation: 针对现有具身导航评估体系在仿真与真机间存在巨大鸿沟、难以真实反映部署风险的问题，旨在建立从基准构建到机器人执行的一致性评估标准。Addressing the huge gap between simulation and real-world deployment in existing embodied navigation evaluation systems, which fails to accurately reflect deployment risks, aiming to establish consistent evaluation standards from benchmark construction to robot execution.
方法: Method: 提出基于单一Trace的跨模态评估框架，强制仿真环境与真实机器人硬件在完全相同的轨迹数据下运行，并引入“可修复性”标准来量化硬件误差与系统鲁棒性。Proposing a single-Trace cross-modal evaluation framework that forces simulation environments and real robot hardware to run on identical trajectory data, introducing a 'repairability' standard to quantify hardware errors and system robustness.
推进: Advance: 揭示了基础设施连续性比单一模型性能提升更能准确预测真实落地风险，证明了多模态数据在连接仿真与真机评估中的关键作用，打破了以往仅依赖仿真分数的评估局限。Revealing that infrastructure continuity is a more accurate predictor of real-world deployment risks than single-model performance improvements, proving the critical role of multi-modal data in connecting simulation and real-world evaluation, and breaking the limitation of relying solely on simulation scores.

Exposing the Long-tail in Embodied Urban Navigation via Scalable Learning from In-the-Wild Videos
动机: Motivation: 为突破现有导航基准数据规模小、场景单一且缺乏长尾分布覆盖的瓶颈，试图利用海量开放网络视频构建高保真、大规模的城市导航训练与评估数据。Aiming to break through the bottlenecks of small-scale, single-scenario existing navigation benchmarks lacking long-tail coverage by utilizing massive open web videos to construct high-fidelity, large-scale urban navigation training and evaluation data.
方法: Method: 构建WILD-Nav基准，利用500小时野外视频数据结合思维链（CoT）机制进行可扩展学习，并通过教师模型诊断长尾失败案例以分析模型在复杂城市环境中的缺陷。Constructing the WILD-Nav benchmark, utilizing 500 hours of field video data combined with a Chain-of-Thought (CoT) mechanism for scalable learning, and using a teacher model to diagnose long-tail failure cases to analyze model deficiencies in complex urban environments.
推进: Advance: 首次系统性地量化并暴露了具身城市导航中的长尾分布问题，证明了开放词汇与大规模多模态数据能显著提升语义泛化能力，同时指出了当前模型在Sim-to-Real迁移中仍存在的根本性误差。Systematically quantifying and exposing the long-tail distribution problem in embodied urban navigation for the first time, proving that open-vocabulary and large-scale multi-modal data significantly enhance semantic generalization, while pointing out fundamental errors still present in Sim-to-Real transfer.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有具身导航基准过度依赖外部阈值终止任务、掩盖模型缺乏真正“自律”与任务理解能力的问题，旨在揭示多模态大模型在知行合一上的根本缺陷。Aiming to address the issue that existing embodied navigation benchmarks overly rely on external thresholds to terminate tasks, thereby masking the models' lack of true 'autonomy' and task understanding capabilities, to reveal the fundamental defects of multimodal large models in the unity of knowledge and action.
方法: Method: 构建DroneCATS架构，将动作空间直接嵌入提示词以无需微调即可评估多模态大模型，并设计统一的多机编队与终止标准测试集。Constructing the DroneCATS architecture, embedding the action space directly into prompts to evaluate multimodal large models without fine-tuning, and designing a unified test set for multi-drone formations and termination criteria.
推进: Advance: 通过实证发现大模型虽懂规则但控制不准、小模型能精准控制却缺乏任务终止意识，暴露了端到端模型在语义泛化与物理交互中的“知行分离”真相。Empirical findings reveal that large models understand rules but lack precise control, while small models achieve precise control but lack task termination awareness, exposing the truth of 'separation of knowledge and action' in end-to-end models regarding semantic generalization and physical interaction.

HitMem: Hierarchical Temporal 3D Memory with Multi-Modal Context-Aware Retrieval for Dynamic Environments
动机: Motivation: 为突破传统具身导航记忆机制仅能处理静态场景、无法应对动态物体交互的瓶颈，致力于构建能够感知物体运动与时间演变的动态记忆系统。To break through the bottleneck where traditional embodied navigation memory mechanisms can only handle static scenes and cannot cope with dynamic object interactions, dedicated to building a dynamic memory system capable of perceiving object motion and temporal evolution.
方法: Method: 提出HitMem分层拓扑记忆架构，将载体与被载物分离并引入时间衰减机制，结合多模态上下文检索实现动态环境下的记忆更新。Proposing the HitMem hierarchical topological memory architecture, separating the carrier and the carried object and introducing a time decay mechanism, combined with multimodal context retrieval to achieve memory updates in dynamic environments.
推进: Advance: 在仿真环境中验证了模型对动态物体（如移动NPC）的追踪与记忆能力，揭示了现有静态基准无法评估的“环境理解与适应”缺陷，推动了从静态规划向动态推理的范式转变。Validated the model's tracking and memory capabilities for dynamic objects (e.g., moving NPCs) in simulation environments, revealing the 'environment understanding and adaptation' defects that existing static benchmarks cannot assess, and promoting the paradigm shift from static planning to dynamic reasoning.

MulDP: Multimodal Diffusion Policy for Autonomous Quadruped Parkour Navigation across Complex Terrains
动机: Motivation: 针对传统控制策略在极端地形下动作分布单一、缺乏长程动量规划能力的问题，旨在利用生成式模型突破多模态数据稀缺带来的泛化瓶颈。Aims to utilize generative models to break through generalization bottlenecks caused by scarce multimodal data, addressing the issue of single action distributions and lack of long-horizon momentum planning in traditional control strategies under extreme terrains.
方法: Method: 提出MulDP框架，利用扩散模型显式建模动作分布的多模态性，并构建包含复杂地形的QPND数据集以支持高保真物理交互训练。Proposes the MulDP framework, explicitly modeling the multimodality of action distributions via diffusion models, and constructing the QPND dataset containing complex terrains to support high-fidelity physical interaction training.
推进: Advance: 在复杂地形上实现了89.7%的成功率，揭示了生成式策略能捕捉“预判加速”等物理本质，为从静态避障转向动态环境适应提供了新范式。Achieved an 89.7% success rate on complex terrains, revealing that generative strategies can capture physical essence behaviors like 'predictive acceleration', providing a new paradigm for shifting from static obstacle avoidance to dynamic environment adaptation.

方法: Method: 构建EgoPathBench基准，通过大规模零样本具身导航任务评估模型在视觉语言提示下的多步 waypoint 决策能力，并开源相关微调数据。We construct the EgoPathBench benchmark to evaluate models' multi-step waypoint decision-making capabilities under visual-language prompts via large-scale zero-shot embodied navigation tasks, and release the associated fine-tuning data.
推进: Advance: 揭示了当前最先进模型在具身路径任务上成功率仅2.9%的严峻现实，证明多步决策中的几何一致性是Foundation Model在空间智商上的根本缺陷。This reveals the severe reality that the success rate of current state-of-the-art models on embodied path tasks is only 2.9%, proving that geometric consistency in multi-step decision-making is a fundamental flaw of Foundation Models in spatial intelligence.

方法: Method: 基于UE5引擎构建DeliveryGym仿真平台，引入自适应课程学习机制，将送货任务建模为包含多阶段决策的长视界强化学习问题。Building the DeliveryGym simulation platform based on the UE5 engine, introducing an adaptive curriculum learning mechanism to model delivery tasks as long-horizon reinforcement learning problems involving multi-stage decision-making.
推进: Advance: 揭示了单纯监督微调无法解决长程规划缺陷，证明通过环境反馈进行强化学习能显著提升模型在资源约束下的策略泛化能力。Revealing that supervised fine-tuning alone cannot resolve long-horizon planning defects, proving that reinforcement learning via environmental feedback can significantly enhance the policy generalization capability of models under resource constraints.

ColoACT: Multi-Cue Action Chunking for Smooth Autonomous Colon Navigation on a Self-Propelled Endoscopic Robot
动机: Motivation: 针对内镜导航中弱纹理、高反光及粘弹性交互导致的感知失效与动作抖动问题，旨在突破单一视觉模态在复杂物理环境下的泛化瓶颈。Aiming to break the generalization bottleneck of single visual modalities in complex physical environments, this work addresses the issues of perception failure and action jitter caused by weak textures, high reflectivity, and viscoelastic interactions in endoscopic navigation.
方法: Method: 提出ColoACT系统，融合RGB、深度图与伪高程图多模态输入，结合时间集成策略对动作分块（Action Chunking）进行平滑处理。The ColoACT system is proposed, which fuses multi-modal inputs of RGB, depth maps, and pseudo-elevation maps, and applies smoothing processing to Action Chunking using a temporal integration strategy.
推进: Advance: 引入梯度高程图显著增强了模型对结肠褶皱特征的语义理解，在离体猪结肠实验中实现了85%的直段导航成功率，验证了多模态融合对提升物理交互鲁棒性的有效性。The introduction of gradient elevation maps significantly enhances the model's semantic understanding of colonic folds features, achieving an 85% straight-segment navigation success rate in ex vivo pig colon experiments, validating the effectiveness of multi-modal fusion in improving physical interaction robustness.
2026-10
Odyssey: A Closed-Loop Benchmark for Long-Horizon Real-World Driving with Explicit Navigation Routes
动机: Motivation: 针对现有端到端驾驶评估局限于短序列（如20秒）导致无法检验长程依赖（如变道后右转）的问题，以及真实世界数据稀缺和指令模糊的瓶颈。Addressing the limitations of existing end-to-end driving evaluations restricted to short sequences (e.g., 20 seconds) which fail to test long-horizon dependencies (e.g., turning right after a lane change), as well as the bottlenecks of scarce real-world data and vague instructions.
方法: Method: 构建基于3D高斯溅射与扩散模型修复的闭环仿真环境，利用100秒长序列和语义地图（SD）替代传统高清地图，以提供高保真且长视野的导航测试场景。Constructing a closed-loop simulation environment based on 3D Gaussian Splatting and diffusion model repair, utilizing 100-second long sequences and semantic maps (SD) to replace traditional high-definition maps, providing high-fidelity and long-horizon navigation test scenarios.
推进: Advance: 将评估窗口显著拉长至100秒，首次系统性检验了模型在长时序任务中的空间记忆与路径规划能力，揭示了模型在缺乏精细车道结构信息下的泛化缺陷。Significantly extending the evaluation window to 100 seconds, systematically testing the model's spatial memory and path planning capabilities in long-temporal tasks for the first time, revealing generalization defects in models lacking fine-grained lane structure information.

记忆机制与长期一致性Memory Mechanisms and Long-term Consistency

本方向聚焦于解决长程导航中的记忆遗忘、循环与状态表征漂移问题。核心思路从单纯依赖几何地图或语义地图，演进为引入动态记忆衰减、反事实世界模型及轨迹级一致性约束。早期工作通过外部地图外包记忆，但受限于VLM幻觉；中期尝试引入世界模型预测环境动态，却面临生成准确率与泛化性挑战；近期研究转向测试时记忆调控（如衰减机制）与轨迹级诊断（如卡尔曼滤波监控），试图在无需重训的前提下，通过几何与语义的双重约束抑制循环与震荡，实现从“记住路径”到“理解路径一致性”的跨越。This direction focuses on addressing memory forgetting, looping, and state representation drift in long-range navigation. The core approach has evolved from relying solely on geometric or semantic maps to incorporating dynamic memory decay, counterfactual world models, and trajectory-level consistency constraints. Early work outsourced memory to external maps but was limited by VLM hallucinations; mid-stage attempts introduced world models to predict environmental dynamics but faced challenges in generation accuracy and generalization; recent research shifts to test-time memory regulation (e.g., decay mechanisms) and trajectory-level diagnostics (e.g., Kalman filter monitoring), aiming to suppress looping and oscillation through dual geometric and semantic constraints without retraining, achieving a leap from 'remembering paths' to 'understanding path consistency.'

方法: Method: 引入外部语义地图作为“外部大脑”，并采用共形预测作为严格考官来约束探索过程。We introduce an external semantic map as an 'external brain' and employ conformal prediction as a strict examiner to constrain the exploration process.

动机: Motivation: 平衡流式导航中长程历史记忆压缩与实时对话响应之间的矛盾，防止记忆过载或丢失。We balance the contradiction between compressing long-range historical memory and responding to real-time dialogue in streaming navigation, preventing memory overload or loss.
方法: Method: 提出SlowFast双速上下文建模，Fast流处理即时交互，Slow流通过体素剪枝压缩历史记忆。We propose SlowFast dual-speed context modeling, where the Fast stream processes immediate interactions and the Slow stream compresses historical memory via voxel pruning.
推进: Advance: 在保持低延迟的同时实现了SOTA性能，但体素剪枝在真实遮挡场景下的鲁棒性仍存疑，属于工程优化的典型。We achieve SOTA performance while maintaining low latency, but the robustness of voxel pruning in real occlusion scenarios remains questionable, representing a typical engineering optimization.

GroundControl: Anticipating Navigation Failures in Vision-Language Agents via Trajectory-Consistent Uncertainty Estimates
动机: Motivation: 解决VLM代理因过度自信而陷入轨迹振荡或停滞，传统动作熵无法捕捉此类导航失败的问题。Addressing the problem where VLM agents, due to overconfidence, fall into trajectory oscillation or stagnation, which traditional action entropy fails to capture.

动机: Motivation: 解决VLN on-policy训练中语义监督缺失，导致代理走偏后无法获得有效反馈的样本效率问题。Addressing the sample efficiency problem in VLN on-policy training where the lack of semantic supervision prevents agents from receiving effective feedback after deviating from the path.
方法: Method: 引入Path-level Hindsight Instructions，利用LVLM为走偏轨迹生成新指令，将错误转化为新样本。We introduce Path-level Hindsight Instructions, utilizing LVLMs to generate new instructions for deviated trajectories, converting errors into new samples.

DART-VLN: Test-Time Memory Decay and Anti-Loop Regularization for Discrete Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 解决现有记忆机制在测试时因“记性太好”导致过时信息干扰，或“原地踏步”产生循环的问题。Addressing the problem where existing memory mechanisms cause interference from outdated information due to 'too good memory' at test time, or produce loops due to 'treading water.'
方法: Method: 提出DART-VLN，通过测试时记忆衰减机制与防回环正则化，轻量级调整旧模型决策。We propose DART-VLN, which uses test-time memory decay mechanisms and anti-loop regularization to lightly adjust the decisions of old models.
推进: Advance: 无需重训即可在R2R等基准上减少步数与时间，证明了外部规则对抑制循环的有效性，但难以解决模型本身表征能力不足的根本问题。We reduce steps and time on benchmarks like R2R without retraining, proving the effectiveness of external rules in suppressing loops, but failing to address the fundamental issue of insufficient model representation capabilities.

Anticipate Before Acting: Future-State-Conditioned Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 针对长程导航中单纯模仿动作导致的记忆遗忘与状态表征漂移问题，旨在通过引入未来状态感知来增强轨迹级的一致性约束。Addresses the issues of memory forgetting and state representation drift caused by purely imitating actions in long-range navigation, aiming to enhance trajectory-level consistency constraints by introducing future state perception.
方法: Method: 在Transformer架构中嵌入两个可学习token分别预知未来与执行当下，训练时对齐专家轨迹远端画面，推理时仅保留预测token作为前缀引导策略。Embeds two learnable tokens within the Transformer architecture to predict the future and execute the present, aligning with expert trajectory far-end frames during training, and retaining only the predicted token as a prefix to guide the policy during inference.
推进: Advance: 以低成本行为克隆变种实现了长程导航成功率提升，证明了无需复杂生成式世界模型即可通过隐式未来信息抑制循环与震荡。Achieves an improvement in long-range navigation success rates through a low-cost behavioral cloning variant, proving that implicit future information can suppress loops and oscillations without requiring complex generative world models.

动机: Motivation: 针对长程导航中传统参数化世界模型难以拟合复杂物理动态且泛化性差的问题，旨在探索一种不依赖显式物理建模的替代方案。Aiming to explore an alternative solution that does not rely on explicit physical modeling, addressing the problem that traditional parametric World Models struggle to fit complex physical dynamics and exhibit poor generalization in long-horizon navigation.
方法: Method: 提出基于检索的多步规划框架，将人类专家的操作录像作为非参数化世界模型，通过检索相似历史轨迹来指导当前决策。Proposing a retrieval-based multi-step planning framework that treats human expert operation videos as a non-parametric World Model, guiding current decisions by retrieving similar historical trajectories.
推进: Advance: 证明了在特定领域利用经验主义检索机制可显著提升长程任务成功率，实现了从单步预测到基于历史一致性的多步规划跨越。Demonstrating that utilizing an empiricism-based retrieval mechanism in specific domains can significantly improve success rates in long-horizon tasks, achieving a leap from single-step prediction to multi-step planning based on historical consistency.

分层架构与混合策略Hierarchical Architectures and Hybrid Strategies

本方向聚焦于通过分层架构与混合策略解决具身导航中语义理解与实时控制的矛盾。核心思路是将高层VLM的语义规划与底层RL/扩散的精细控制解耦，或利用传统几何方法作为“骨架”以弥补纯学习方法的泛化缺陷。演进脉络从简单的端到端尝试转向“慢脑快手”的协同模式：先由VLM进行长程语义推理，再由高效规划器或流场执行局部动作。这种架构既保留了大模型的常识推理能力，又通过几何约束或专用控制器确保了实时性与安全性，有效平衡了性能与计算开销，推动导航从仿真走向真实复杂环境。This direction focuses on resolving the contradiction between semantic understanding and real-time control in embodied navigation through hierarchical architectures and hybrid strategies. The core approach decouples high-level VLM semantic planning from low-level RL/diffusion fine control, or employs traditional geometric methods as a 'skeleton' to compensate for the generalization defects of pure learning methods. The evolution moves from simple end-to-end attempts to a 'slow brain, fast hands' collaborative mode: first, VLMs perform long-range semantic reasoning, followed by efficient planners or flow fields executing local actions. This architecture retains the common sense reasoning capabilities of large models while ensuring real-time performance and safety through geometric constraints or dedicated controllers, effectively balancing performance and computational overhead, and driving navigation from simulation to real-world complex environments.

不确定性感知与概率推断Uncertainty Awareness and Probabilistic Inference

在导航领域引入不确定性感知与概率推断，旨在解决传统确定性方法在复杂动态环境中因传感器噪声、遮挡及多模态歧义导致的定位失效问题。核心思路是从输出单一最优轨迹转向建模状态空间的后验分布，显式量化认知盲区。演进路径上，早期工作多依赖卡尔曼滤波处理线性高斯噪声，而近期研究（如MAGNETAR）利用深度生成模型直接推断联合概率分布，能够捕捉多模态解（如多路径效应）并区分感知模糊与系统误差，显著提升了机器人在非结构化环境中的鲁棒性与决策安全性。Introducing uncertainty awareness and probabilistic inference into the navigation field to address localization failures caused by sensor noise, occlusion, and multi-modal ambiguity in complex dynamic environments by traditional deterministic methods. The core idea is to shift from outputting a single optimal trajectory to modeling the posterior distribution of the state space, explicitly quantifying cognitive blind spots. In terms of evolution, early work relied heavily on Kalman filters to handle linear Gaussian noise, while recent research (e.g., MAGNETAR) utilizes deep generative models to directly infer joint probability distributions, capable of capturing multi-modal solutions (e.g., multi-path effects) and distinguishing between perceptual ambiguity and system errors, significantly enhancing robot robustness and decision safety in unstructured environments.

2026-09

动机: Motivation: 针对传统射频定位方法在杂乱环境中强行输出虚假高置信度单点估计，导致对发射源朝向和位置误判的问题，亟需一种能诚实反映感知不确定性的机制。Addressing the problem where traditional RF localization methods force the output of false high-confidence point estimates in cluttered environments, leading to misjudgments of transmitter orientation and position, necessitating a mechanism that honestly reflects perceptual uncertainty.
方法: Method: 提出MAGNETAR框架，不再追求点估计，而是利用深度网络直接推断包含位置和朝向的联合空间后验分布（Spatial Posterior），以捕捉信号传播中的多路径效应和方向性盲区。Proposing the MAGNETAR framework, which no longer pursues point estimates but instead uses deep networks to directly infer the joint spatial posterior distribution (Spatial Posterior) containing both position and orientation, to capture multi-path effects and directional blind spots in signal propagation.
推进: Advance: 突破了单一解假设的局限，显式建模了多模态不确定性，证明了在信号弱或遮挡场景下，概率分布比单点估计更能真实反映环境状态，为后续概率导航决策提供了更可靠的状态输入。Breaking through the limitations of the single-solution assumption, explicitly modeling multi-modal uncertainty, and proving that in scenarios with weak signals or occlusion, probability distributions reflect the environmental state more truthfully than point estimates, providing more reliable state inputs for subsequent probabilistic navigation decisions.
