Performance-Preserving Online Adaptation in Social Navigation via Diffusion Steering
动机: Motivation: 针对社交导航中扩散策略微调易破坏原有避障鲁棒性、导致不确定性量化失效的问题，旨在解决策略适应新环境时的认知盲区风险。Aiming to address the risk of cognitive blind spots when strategies adapt to new environments, this work targets the problem where fine-tuning diffusion policies in social navigation easily undermines existing obstacle avoidance robustness, leading to the failure of uncertainty quantification.
方法: Method: 提出基于扩散引导的在线适应框架，在固定扩散策略的潜空间内通过强化学习调整噪声分布而非直接修改权重，以显式建模社交交互的不确定性。Proposes an online adaptation framework based on diffusion guidance, adjusting noise distributions via reinforcement learning within the latent space of a fixed diffusion policy rather than directly modifying weights, to explicitly model the uncertainty of social interactions.
推进: Advance: 实现了在保持原有高置信度避障能力前提下的在线社会礼仪适应，证明了通过操控噪声空间而非策略参数能更稳健地处理多模态社交歧义。Achieves online social etiquette adaptation while maintaining existing high-confidence obstacle avoidance capabilities, demonstrating that manipulating the noise space rather than strategy parameters can more robustly handle multimodal social ambiguities.
2026-09
AquaOrbit: Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Underwater Target Orbiting under Intermittent Visual Feedback
动机: Motivation: 解决水下视觉传感器在动态遮挡下产生的感知不确定性导致定位失效及策略“失忆”问题，需量化并处理观测缺失带来的状态分布模糊。Addresses the issue of localization failure and strategy 'amnesia' caused by perception uncertainty generated by underwater visual sensors under dynamic occlusion, requiring quantification and processing of state distribution ambiguity brought by observation loss.
方法: Method: 设计了一种结合姿态锁存机制与强化学习的Sim-to-Real控制器，利用历史状态先验在视觉丢失期间维持对目标轨道的概率推断。Designs a Sim-to-Real controller combining a pose-locking mechanism with reinforcement learning, utilizing historical state priors to maintain probabilistic inference on the target trajectory during visual loss.
推进: Advance: 验证了在长达8秒的完全视觉遮挡下，基于历史状态推断的鲁棒性显著优于传统PID，为非结构化水下环境中的感知不确定性提供了有效的短期补偿策略。Validates that robustness based on historical state inference significantly outperforms traditional PID under up to 8 seconds of complete visual occlusion, providing an effective short-term compensation strategy for perception uncertainty in unstructured underwater environments.
2026-09
Credit-Guided Policy Improvement for Test-time Adaptive Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 现有测试时适应方法盲目优化策略，缺乏对动作有效性的量化评估，导致在不确定性高的环境中出现策略漂移。Existing test-time adaptation methods blindly optimize strategies without quantitative assessment of action effectiveness, leading to policy drift in high-uncertainty environments.
方法: Method: 提出信用引导策略改进机制，通过观测状态变化计算‘参考相对决策信用’，仅对高信用动作进行更新，低信用则回滚。Proposing a credit-guided strategy improvement mechanism that calculates 'reference relative decision credit' based on observed state changes, updating only high-credit actions while rolling back low-credit ones.
推进: Advance: 将策略改进与状态变化解耦，显式量化了决策的可靠性，避免了传统熵最小化方法在模糊环境下的盲目适应。Decoupling strategy improvement from state changes to explicitly quantify decision reliability, avoiding the blind adaptation of traditional entropy minimization methods in ambiguous environments.
2026-09
Seek Before You Move: Evidence Seeking for Progress Grounding in Vision-Language Navigation
动机: Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航模型存在‘进展近视症’，在感知证据不足时过度自信地执行动作，导致在复杂路口频繁迷失。Existing Visual Language Navigation models suffer from 'progress myopia', exhibiting overconfidence in executing actions when perceptual evidence is insufficient, leading to frequent disorientation at complex intersections.
方法: Method: 引入‘先寻找后移动’的主动探索机制，强制模型在不确定性高时执行证据搜索动作以获取关键信息，再进行路径规划。Introducing an 'explore before move' active exploration mechanism that forces the model to execute evidence search actions to acquire key information when uncertainty is high, before proceeding with path planning.
推进: Advance: 将被动推理转化为主动不确定性感知，通过显式建模认知盲区显著提升了复杂环境下的导航成功率与鲁棒性。Transforming passive reasoning into active uncertainty perception, significantly improving navigation success rates and robustness in complex environments by explicitly modeling cognitive blind spots.
2026-10
Optimal Transport Meets Reinforcement Learning: A Survey
动机: Motivation: 传统导航中基于KL或JS散度的分布优化在状态分布不重叠时梯度失效，导致机器人难以在复杂动态环境中有效量化感知不确定性。In traditional navigation, distribution optimization based on KL or JS divergence fails in gradients when state distributions do not overlap, making it difficult for robots to effectively quantify perception uncertainty in complex dynamic environments.
方法: Method: 引入最优传输（Optimal Transport）理论替代传统散度，利用几何成本构建分布间的梯度，并结合强化学习框架处理奖励信号与信任域约束。Optimal Transport (OT) theory is introduced to replace traditional divergences, using geometric costs to construct gradients between distributions, and combined with reinforcement learning frameworks to handle reward signals and trust region constraints.
推进: Advance: 解决了分布重叠弱时的梯度消失问题，使算法能在多模态解（如多路径歧义）存在时依然提供有效的概率推断更新，显著提升了非结构化环境下的鲁棒性。It solves the gradient vanishing problem when distribution overlap is weak, allowing the algorithm to provide effective probabilistic inference updates even when multi-modal solutions (such as multi-path ambiguity) exist, significantly enhancing robustness in unstructured environments.
2026-10
Cross-entropy optimization with prioritized constraints
动机: Motivation: 在不确定性感知中，传统方法难以在动态约束下平衡安全关键任务与系统误差，导致概率推断在复杂约束下失效。In uncertainty perception, traditional methods struggle to balance safety-critical tasks and system errors under dynamic constraints, leading to the failure of probabilistic inference under complex constraints.
方法: Method: 提出TierCEM算法，为交叉熵优化引入优先级过滤器，按约束优先级顺序筛选轨迹，优先满足高优先级安全约束。The TierCEM algorithm is proposed, which introduces a priority filter for cross-entropy optimization to screen trajectories in order of constraint priority, prioritizing the satisfaction of high-priority safety constraints.
推进: Advance: 将概率推断从单纯的最优解搜索转化为带层级约束的鲁棒决策，显式区分了感知模糊与系统误差对安全的影响，提升了在动态环境中的决策安全性。Probabilistic inference is transformed from a simple optimal solution search into robust decision-making with hierarchical constraints, explicitly distinguishing the impact of perception ambiguity and system errors on safety, thereby improving decision safety in dynamic environments.
2026-10
EpicWorldModel: Exploration-driven Planning with Latent World Models
动机: Motivation: 针对传统确定性世界模型在视觉遮挡等部分可观测场景下产生幻觉且无法量化认知盲区的问题，旨在通过显式建模多模态不确定性来提升导航鲁棒性。Aiming to enhance navigation robustness by explicitly modeling multimodal uncertainty, addressing the issue where traditional deterministic World Models generate hallucinations and cannot quantify cognitive blind spots in partially observable scenarios like visual occlusion.
方法: Method: 引入 Flow Matching 机制替代单点回归，将 JEPA 架构改造为概率生成模型，使其能够采样并推断多种潜在的未来状态轨迹。Introducing a Flow Matching mechanism to replace single-point regression, transforming the JEPA architecture into a probabilistic generative model capable of sampling and inferring multiple potential future state trajectories.
推进: Advance: 成功捕捉了多路径效应等复杂环境下的多模态解，在视觉受限的迷宫导航任务中比确定性基线提升了 22% 的性能。Successfully capturing multimodal solutions in complex environments such as multi-path effects, improving performance by 22% over deterministic baselines in maze navigation tasks with visual constraints.
2026-10
Reachability-Aware Diffusion Policy Optimization
动机: Motivation: 为了解决传统扩散策略在动态导航中仅依赖累积成本反馈而缺乏前瞻性风险感知，导致无法有效规避潜在碰撞的问题。Addressing the issue where traditional diffusion policies in dynamic navigation rely solely on cumulative cost feedback, lacking forward-looking risk perception, and thus failing to effectively avoid potential collisions.
方法: Method: 提出基于“首次命中”时间的可达性预测机制，将不确定性风险量化为安全预算反馈，以此调节扩散策略的优化目标。Proposing an reachability prediction mechanism based on 'time-to-first-hit', quantifying uncertainty risk as a safety budget feedback to regulate the optimization objective of diffusion policies.
推进: Advance: 使机器人从被动避障转变为主动“绕道”，在无需严格拉格朗日约束的情况下实现了更灵活且前瞻性的安全导航决策。Enabling robots to shift from passive obstacle avoidance to active 'detouring', achieving more flexible and forward-looking safe navigation decisions without strict Lagrangian constraints.
2026-10
RT-Safe: Benchmarking Agent Safety in Real-Time Embodied Environment
动机: Motivation: 现有具身智能基准往往假设环境静态或忽略推理延迟，导致高任务完成率掩盖了动态环境下的碰撞风险，无法量化感知与决策中的不确定性。Existing embodied intelligence benchmarks often assume static environments or ignore inference latency, leading to high task completion rates that mask collision risks in dynamic environments and failing to quantify uncertainty in perception and decision-making.
方法: Method: 构建RT-SAFE基准，在实时动态环境中评估代理，通过引入物理交互（如行人变道）和测量推理时间对安全的影响，揭示延迟即风险的物理事实。Constructing the RT-SAFE benchmark to evaluate agents in real-time dynamic environments, revealing the physical fact that latency equals risk by introducing physical interactions (such as pedestrian lane changes) and measuring the impact of inference time on safety.
推进: Advance: 发现增加推理时间反而增加碰撞概率，打破了“思考越久越安全”的直觉，证明了在动态环境中显式建模时间不确定性对安全决策的必要性。Discovering that increasing inference time actually increases collision probability, breaking the intuition that 'thinking longer means safer' and proving the necessity of explicitly modeling temporal uncertainty for safe decision-making in dynamic environments.
